## kinsn project entry and RFC design

| Old path | New path |
| --- | --- |
| `kinsn/docs/design.md` (paper-prototype hub) | `kinsn/docs/archive/paper-prototype-design.md` |

The current `kinsn/docs/design.md` describes the separate upstream RFC.

## Renames

`Kops`, `KOperation`, `kop` → `kinsn`; `KOP` → `KINSN`; `Kop` → `Kinsn`; `kopprober` → `kinsnprober`. Unrelated socket, Unicode and upstream cryptography names are unchanged. Immutable external paper/dependency URLs and the old columns in these tables preserve their original spelling. Directory mappings apply to every descendant with its relative path unchanged.
